zoyon patrol	v	1987		0	43	3	Zoyon Patrol was an early educational simulation game, produced by MECC for the Apple II computer. In the game, the player is the director of the Zoyon patrol, located on the fictional Zaphyr Island. A Zoyon is a fictional creature, whose names and features are combination of pre-existing creatures.
